BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                      業務回顧
On behalf of the board of directors (the “Board”), I am delighted to report the      本人謹代表董事會（「董事會」），欣然向各位股
annual results of Kingboard Laminates Holdings Limited and its subsidiaries          東公佈，建滔積層板控股有限公司及其附屬公
(the “Group”) for the financial year ended 31 December 2023 (the “Period”).          司（「集團」）截至二零二三年十二月三十一日止
                                                                                     財政年度（「回顧期」）之全年業績。

During the Period, geopolitical conflicts persisted while inflation and              於回顧期內，地緣政治衝突持續，加上通脹率
interest rates remained high, leading to weak consumer confidence and                及利率高企，消費信心疲弱，整體電子市場需
overall subdued demand in the electronics market. In addition, the overall           求低迷，加上前兩年覆銅面板產能的擴張，致
expansion in laminates production capacity over the previous two years has           使產能過剩。雖然覆銅面板市場競爭激烈，但
led to excess capacity in the industry. Despite the resulting intense market         有賴集團擁有完善的垂直整合產業鏈，加上不
competition, the Group has successfully leveraged its vertically integrated          斷的技術改良以及出色的成本控制提升市場競
supply chain, continuous technological enhancements and stringent cost               爭力，同時集團積極開拓新客戶及全力推動終
control to strengthen its competitive position. Together with active new             端客户認証，目前擁有更加龐大及多元化業務
business development and end customer certification, the Group now                   的客戶網絡，在市況疲弱下，年內的出貨量仍
commands a broader and more diversified clientele network, enabling                  能較去年增長3%。但主要由於覆銅面板市場銷
the Group to achieve a 3% increase in annual sales volume. However,                  售單價下跌，覆銅面板部門的營業額較去年同
segment revenue of the Laminates Division was adversely affected by the              期下跌。房地產部門則因可銷售餘貨減少，部
general decline in laminate market’s selling prices. As the remaining saleable       門營業額下降。
properties diminished, the Property Division recorded a decline in revenue.

PERFORMANCE                                                                         業務表現
Laminates Division: The Period saw the electronics industry facing a                覆銅面板部門： 回顧期內，電子行業需求下
decline in demand, but the Laminates Division made satisfactory progress            滑，但覆銅面板部門積極拓展新的市場領
with its product portfolio enhancement efforts as it actively pursued growth        域，產品組合優化取得理想進展，用於便攜
in new market arenas. There was a continued rise in the sales of high-              式設備的薄板、符合高環保標準的無鉛無鹵
end and high-value-added products. This high-end portfolio includes thin            素覆銅面板、擁有廣泛環境適應度的耐燃覆
laminates designed for portable devices, lead-free and halogen-free laminates       銅面板以及低損耗高傳輸速度的高頻高速覆
complying with stringent environmental standards, and fire-resistant laminates      銅面板等高端、高附加值產品銷售數量持續
with wide compatibility, as well as high-frequency and high-speed laminates         增 加 。 覆 銅 面 板 部 門 營 業 額 下 降 14% ， 至
ensuring low loss and high transmission speed. Segment revenue of the               一百六十四億四千七百八十萬港元。回顧期
Laminates Division declined 14% to HK$16,447.8 million. Fierce competition          內，行業競爭激烈，產品價格降幅大於生產成
in the industry during the Period, with selling prices dropping more steeply        本降幅，毛利率同比下降，不少業界同行於二
than production costs, resulted in a year-on-year decrease in gross                 零二三年均錄得虧損。有賴集團經驗豐富的
profit margin, leading many industry participants to incur losses in 2023.          管理團隊不斷改進生產技術來提升生產效率及
Nevertheless, with its experienced management team’s continuous efforts             降低能耗，同時透過提升生產設備自動化率減
to raise efficiency and reduce energy consumption through technological             省人員開支，有效地緩衝毛利率下滑帶來的壓
enhancements, and to achieve labour cost savings by increasing automation           力，未扣除利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前之利潤
at its facilities, the Group has effectively buffered the pressure brought by the   下降21%，至二十四億零二百三十萬港元。
decline in gross profit margin. Earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation
and amortisation (“EBITDA”) were down 21% to HK$2,402.3 million.

Property Division: As the Group continued to focus on the development of            地產部門： 集團繼續執行專注覆銅面板業務
its laminates business, the Property Division was mainly engaged in the sales       的發展策略，地產部門年內主要以銷售餘貨
of the remaining units. As the number of remaining saleable units diminished,       為主，因可銷售餘貨減少，部門營業額下降
segment revenue dropped by 93% to HK$226.8 million. EBITDA also down                93%，至二億二千六百八十萬港元，未扣除利
96% to HK$57.9 million.                                                             息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前之利潤下降96%，至

According to above announcements, GRFP were not able to pay the bond                  根據上述之公告，由於其惡劣財務狀況，廣州
interests due to its adverse financial condition. The Group had performed an          富力地產故此未能支付其債券利息。本集團已
impairment assessment on the debt instruments held under the expected                 根據預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模型對所
credit loss (“ECL”) model. The measurement of ECL is a function of the                持有之債務工具進行減值評估。預期信貸虧損
probability of default and loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if      之計量是違約概率及違約損失率（即發生違約時
there is a default), with the assessment of the probability of default and loss       的損失程度）的函數，而違約概率及違約損失率
given default being based on historical data and forward-looking information.         乃基於歷史數據及前瞻性資料評估。預期信貸
The estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-weighted amount            虧損的估計反映無偏頗及概率加權金額，該金
that is determined with the respective risks of default occurring as the              額以發生違約的相應風險為權重釐定，同時亦
weights, and also with reference to the time value of money. In determining           參考貨幣的時間價值。於釐定本集團於年內之
ECL on the Group’s debt instruments for the year, the management had                  債務工具的預期信貸虧損時，管理層考慮各種
taken into accounts factors including the defaults of the bond issuer in              因素，包括債券發行人拖欠其債務的利息及本
making payments of interest and principal for its indebtedness, and forward-          金付款以及前瞻性資料，包括債券發行人現時
looking information including the future macroeconomic conditions at places           經營所在地的未來宏觀經濟狀況。
where the bond issuer is operating.

The Group recognised impairment loss under ECL model of HK$270,000                    經參考違約風險、回收率及前瞻性資料之調整
in profit or loss for the reporting period and the accumulated ECL of                 後，本集團就以上之廣州富力地產按公平值計
HK$186,771,000 on the above GRFP debt instruments at fair value through               入其他全面收益之債務工具於報告期內於損
other comprehensive income by reference to exposures at default, recovery             益內按預期信貸虧損模式項下確認減值虧損
rate and adjustments for forward looking information.                                 270,000港元，累計信貸虧損為186,771,000港

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT
企業管治報告

The board of directors (the “Board”) of the Company recognises the                 本公司董事會（「董事會」）明暸上市公司企業管
importance of corporate governance practice of a listed company. It is in          治常規之重要性。上市公司之營運具透明度，
the interest of the stakeholders and shareholders for a listed company to          採納各種自行規管政策與程序以及監控機制，
operate in a transparent manner with the adoptions of various self-regulatory      並清楚界定董事與管理層權責，乃符合權益持
policies, procedures and monitoring mechanisms and a clear definition of           有人及股東之利益。
accountability of directors and management.

The Company has adopted the principles of corporate governance and                 本公司於截至二零二三年十二月三十一日止年
complied with the code provisions as set out in the Corporate Governance           度年內已採納企業管治原則並一直遵守香港聯

     Risk Management, Internal Control and Responsibility                              風險管理、內部監控及董事會責
     of the Board                                                                      任
     The Board acknowledges its accountability to the risk management                  董事會承認其須對風險管理及內部監控
     and internal control systems and its responsibility to review the                 制度負責，並有責任檢討該制度之效
     effectiveness of the systems. The Board also clarifies that the system            益。董事會亦闡釋該制度旨在管理而非
     are purported to manage, but not eliminate, the risk of failure to                消除未能達成業務目標的風險，而且只
     fulfil business objectives, and can only provide reasonable but not               能就不會有重大的失實陳述或損失作出
     absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.                         合理而非絕對的保證。

     Management is responsible for setting an appropriate tone from                    管理層負責從頂層為監控定調、執行風
     the top, performing risk assessments, and owning the design,                      險評估、並設計、實施及維護內部監
     implementation and maintenance of internal control. Essential to such             控。對風險管理及內部監控制度不可或
     risk management and internal control systems are well defined policies            缺的是明確妥善訂立書面政策和程序，
     and procedures that are properly documented and communicated to                   並向員工傳達。
     employees.

     The Group has in place a system of internal controls, the key elements            本集團已設立內部監控制度，其主要要
     of which are as follows:                                                          點如下：

1.    GENERAL                                                                         1.   一般事項
      Kingboard Laminates Holdings Limited (the “Company”) was                              建滔積層板控股有限公司（「本公司」）在
      incorporated and registered as an exempted company with limited                       開曼群島註冊成立並登記為一家獲豁
      liability in the Cayman Islands and its shares are listed on the Main                 免之有限公司，其股份於香港聯合交
      Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                          易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。
      Exchange”). Its immediate parent company is Jamplan (BVI) Limited,                    本公司之直屬母公司為Jamplan (BVI)
      a limited liability company incorporated in the British Virgin Islands and            Limited.，該公司為一家於英屬處女群
      in the opinion of the directors of the Company (the “Directors”), its                 島註冊成立之有限公司，而本公司董事
      ultimate holding company is Kingboard Holdings Limited (“KHL”), an                   （「董事」）認為，本公司之最終控股公司
      exempted limited liability company incorporated in the Cayman Islands                 為建滔集團有限公司（「建滔集團」，該
      with its shares also listed on the Main Board of the Stock Exchange.                  公司為一家於開曼群島註冊成立並獲豁
      The addresses of the registered office and principal place of business                免之有限公司，其股份於聯交所主板上
      of the Company are disclosed in the section headed “Corporate                         市。本公司註冊辦事處之地址及主要營
      Information” of the annual report.                                                    業地點於本年報「公司資料」一節中披
                                                                                            露。

      The Company is an investment holding company and the principal                       本公司為投資控股公司，其主要附屬公
      activities of its principal subsidiaries are set out in Note 40.                     司之主要業務載於附註40。

      The consolidated financial statements are presented in Hong Kong                     綜合財務報表以港元（「港元」）呈列，港
      dollars (“HK$”), which is different from the Company’s functional                    元有別於本公司的功能貨幣人民幣（「人
      currency of Renminbi (“RMB”), the currency of the primary economic                   民幣」），即本公司經營所在的主要經濟
      environment in which the Company operates. Because the                               環境的貨幣。由於本公司股份於聯交所
      Company’s shares are listed on the Stock Exchange, where most                        上市，其大部分投資者均位於香港，因
      of its investors are located in Hong Kong, therefore the Directors                   此董事認為以港元呈列綜合財務報表更
      considered it is more relevant convenient for the users of the                       方便綜合財務報表的使用者。
      consolidated financial statements to present the consolidated financial

2.   APPLICATION OF NEW AND                                                        2.   應用新訂及經修訂之香
     AMENDMENTS TO HONG KONG                                                            港財務報告準則（「香港
     FINANCIAL REPORTING STANDARDS                                                      財務報告準則」）
                                                                                               （續）
     (“HKFRSS”) (continued)
     New and amendments to HKFRSs that are mandatorily                                  於本年度已強制生效之新訂及經
     effective for the current year (continued)                                         修訂香港財務報告準則（續）
     Impacts on application of Amendments to HKAS 1 and HKFRS                           應用香港會計準則第1號（修訂本）及香
     “Practice Statement 2 Disclosure of Accounting Policies”                           港財務報告準則「實務聲明第2號會計政
                                                                                        策的披露」
                                                                                            （修訂本）之影響
     The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in the                     本集團於本年度首次應用該等修訂本。
     current year. HKAS 1 “Presentation of Financial Statements” is                     香港會計準則第1號「財務報表的呈列」
     amended to replace all instances of the term “significant accounting               經修訂，以「重大會計政策資料」取代
     policies” with “material accounting policy information”. Accounting                「重要會計政策」一詞的所有情況。倘會
     policy information is material if, when considered together with                   計政策資料與實體財務報表所載其他資
     other information included in an entity’s financial statements, it can             料一併考慮時，可能合理預期影響一般
     reasonably be expected to influence decisions that the primary users               用途財務報表的主要使用者基於該等財
     of general purpose financial statements make on the basis of those                 務報表作出之決定，則該等資料屬重
     financial statements.                                                              大。

     The amendments also clarify that accounting policy information may                 該修訂本亦釐清，儘管有關款項並不重
     be material because of the nature of the related transactions, other               大，但由於相關交易、其他事項或狀況
     events or conditions, even if the amounts are immaterial. However,                 之性質，故會計政策資料可能屬重大。
     not all accounting policy information relating to material transactions,           然而，並非所有與重大交易、其他事項
     other events or conditions is itself material. If an entity chooses to             或狀況有關之會計政策資料本身屬重
     disclose immaterial accounting policy information, such information                大。倘一間實體選擇披露非重大會計政
     must not obscure material accounting policy information.                           策，則有關資料不得掩蓋重大會計政策
                                                                                        資料。

     HKFRS Practice Statement 2 “Making Materiality Judgements” (the                    香港財務報告準則實務聲明第2號作出
     “Practice Statement”) is also amended to illustrate how an entity                  重大性判斷（「實務聲明」）亦經修訂，以
     applies the “four-step materiality process” to accounting policy                   說明實體如何將「四步法評估重大性流
     disclosures and to judge whether information about an accounting                   程」應用於會計政策披露及可判斷有關
     policy is material to its financial statements. Guidance and examples              會計政策之資料對其財務報表而言是否
     are added to the Practice Statement.                                               屬重大。實務聲明已附加指引及實例。

     The application of the amendments has had no material impact on                    應用該等修訂對本集團的財務狀況及表
     the Group’s financial positions and performance but has affected the               現並無產生重大影響，惟已影響綜合財
     disclosure of the Group’s accounting policies set out in Note 3 to the             務報表附註3所載本集團會計政策的披
     consolidated financial statements.                                                 露。

            Revenue from contracts with customers                                          客戶合約收益
            The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                        本集團於履行履約義務時（或就
            obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or                   此）確認收益，即與特定履約義
            services underlying the particular performance obligation is                   務相關的貨品或服務的「控制權」
            transferred to the customer.                                                   轉移予客戶時確認收益。

            A performance obligation represents a good or service (or                      履約責任指一項明確貨品或服務
            a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of                 （或一批貨品或服務）或一系列大
            distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.                    致相同之明確貨品或服務。

            Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised                     倘符合下列其中一項標準，貨品
            over time by reference to the progress towards complete                        或服務的控制權在一段時間內轉
            satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one of the              移，而我們會按一段時間內已完
            following criteria is met:                                                     成相關履約責任的進度確認收
                                                                                           益：

            •       the customer simultaneously receives and consumes                      •   客戶同時收取及耗用由
                    the benefits provided by the Group’s performance as                        本集團履約所帶來的利
                    the Group performs;                                                        益；

            •       the Group’s performance creates or enhances                            •   本集團履約導致創建或
                    an asset that the customer controls as the Group                           提升於本集團履約時由
                    performs; or                                                               客戶控制的資產；或

            •       the Group’s performance does not create an asset                       •   本集團履約並無產生對
                    with an alternative use to the Group and the Group                         本集團有替代用途的資
                    has an enforceable right to payment for performance                        產，且本集團可享有強
                    completed to date.                                                         制執行權，以收回至今
                                                                                               已履約部分的款項。

            Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the                   否則，營業額於客戶獲得明確商
            customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.                      品或服務的控制權時確認。

            Foreign currencies                                                               外幣
            In preparing the financial statements of each individual group                   編製各個別集團實體之財務報表
            entity, transactions in currencies other than the functional                     時，以該實體之功能貨幣以外的
            currency of that entity (foreign currencies) are recognised                      貨幣（外幣）進行之交易乃按於交
            at the rates of exchanges prevailing on the dates of the                         易日期當時之匯率確認。於報告
            transactions. At the end of the reporting period, monetary                       期間結束當日，以外幣列值之貨
            items denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at                      幣項目以該日之匯率重新匯兌。
            the rates prevailing at that date. Non-monetary items carried                    按公平值列賬且按外幣列值之非
            at fair value that are denominated in foreign currencies are                     貨幣項目乃按釐定公平值當日匯
            retranslated at the rates prevailing on the date when the                        率重新匯兌。當非貨幣項目的公
            fair value was determined. When a fair value gain or loss                        平值收益或虧損於損益中確認
            on a non-monetary item is recognised in profit or loss, any                      時，該收益或虧損的任何匯兌部
            exchange component of that gain or loss is also recognised                       分亦於損益中確認。當非貨幣項
            in profit or loss. When a fair value gain or loss on a non-                      目的公平值收益或虧損於其他全
            monetary item is recognised in other comprehensive                               面收益中確認時，該收益或虧損
            income, any exchange component of that gain or loss is also                      的任何匯兌部分亦於其他全面收
            recognised in other comprehensive income. Non-monetary                           益中確認。以外幣按歷史成本計
            items that are measured in terms of historical cost in a foreign                 量之非貨幣項目不予重新匯兌。
            currency are not retranslated.

             The effective interest method is a method of calculating                       實際利率法是一種計算金融資產

             and of allocating interest income and interest expense over                    分派利息收入及利息支出之方
             the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate                   法。實際利率是於金融資產或金
             that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                      融負債的估計可使用年期或（如
             payments (including all fees and points paid or received that                  適用）更短期間將估計未來現金
             form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction              收入及付款（包括所有已付或已
             costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected                    收並構成實際利率一部分之費
             life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where                  用、交易成本及其他溢價或折
             appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on                   價）精確折現至初步確認時之賬
             initial recognition.                                                           面淨值之比率。

             Interest income from debt instruments at fair value through                    於本集團日常業務過程中來自按
             other comprehensive income (“FVTOCI”) and dividend income                      公平值計入其他全面收益之債務
             from equity instruments at FVTPL which are derived from                        工具的利息收入及來自按公平值
             the Group’s ordinary course of business are presented as                       計入損益之權益工具的股息收入

                    Despite the aforegoing, the Group assumes that the                               儘管存在上述情況，倘
                    credit risk on a debt instrument has not increased                               債務工具於報告日期只
                    significantly since initial recognition if the debt                              具有低信貸風險，本集

                    the reporting date. A debt instrument is determined                              風險自初始確認起並未

                    (ii) the borrower has a strong capacity to meet its                              風險偏低，（ii）借方有強

                    and (iii) adverse changes in economic and business                               約現金流量責任，及（iii）

                    necessarily, reduce the ability of the borrower to                               況存在不利變動，未必
                    fulfil its contractual cash flow obligations. The Group                          削弱借方履行其合約現
                    considers a debt instrument to have low credit                                   金流量義務的能力，則
                    risk when it has an internal or external credit rating                           債務工具的信貸風險會

             Financial guarantee contracts                                                      財務擔保合約
             A financial guarantee contract is a contract that requires the                     財務擔保合約是一種要求發行人

             instrument.                                                                        失的合約。

             Financial guarantee contract liabilities are measured initially at                 財務擔保合約負債按公平值初步

5.    REVENUE (continued)                                                          5.   營業額（續）
      The revenue of the Group arising from sales of glass epoxy laminates,             本集團自銷售環氧玻璃纖維覆銅面板、
      paper laminates, upstream materials and specialty resin is recognised             紙覆銅面板、上游物料及特種樹脂所產
      at a point in time. Under the transfer-of-control approach in HKFRS               生的營業額乃於某一時間點確認。根據
      15, revenue from these sales is recognised when the goods have                    香港財務報告準則第15號的控制權轉移
      been delivered, which is the point of time when the customer has the              法，該等銷售收益乃於獲得交付貨物時
      ability to direct the use of these products and obtain substantially all          確認，即當客戶有能力主導該等產品的
      of the remaining benefits of these products.                                      使用及獲得該等產品絕大部分餘下利益
                                                                                        的時間點。

      The revenue of the Group arising from sales of properties in the                  本集團於日常業務過程中銷售物業產生
      ordinary course of business is recognised at a point in time when the             的營業額於客戶獲得有關物業的控制權
      customer obtains control of the respective properties.                            之時間點予以確認。

      Revenue from hotel accommodation is recognised over time, as                      酒店住宿營業額隨時間確認，因為客戶
      the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the benefits                    隨本集團履約同時收取及耗用由本集團
      provided by the Group’s performance as the Group performs.                        履約所帶來的利益。

      Revenue from rental income from operating leases is recognised on                 來自經營租賃的租金收入按直線法於相
      a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant lease agreement,              關租賃協議年期內確認，其源自本集團
      which is derived from the Group’s ordinary course of business.                    的日常業務過程。

      Transaction price allocated to the remaining                                      分配至餘下客戶合約履約責任的
      performance obligation for contracts with customers                               交易價格
      Apart for sales of properties, all contracts have an original expected            除銷售物業外，所有合約原預期年期均
      duration of one year or less. As permitted under HKFRS 15, the                    為一年或以下。誠如香港財務報告準則
      transaction price allocated to these unsatisfied contracts is not                 第15號所允許，分配至該等未完成合約
      disclosed.                                                                        的交易價格不予披露。

      The transaction price allocated to the remaining performance                      於二零二三年及二零二二年十二月
      obligations for sales of properties (unsatisfied or partially unsatisfied)        三十一日，銷售物業分配至餘下履約責
      as at 31 December 2023 and 2022 and the expected timing of                        任（未達成或部分未達成）的交易價格及
      recognising revenue are as follows:                                               預期確認營業額時間如下：

6.    SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                         6.   分部資料
      HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments” (“HKFRS 8”) requires operating                       香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 8 號「 經 營 分 部 」
      segments to be identified on the basis of internal reports about                 （「香港財務報告準則第8號」）要求以主
      components of the Group that are regularly reviewed by Chief                      要營運決策者（「主要營運決策者」）在對
      Operating Decision Maker (“CODM”) in order to allocate resources to               分部作資源分配及評估其表現上所定期
      segments and to assess their performance. Specifically, the Group’s               審閱的有關集團不同部門之內部報告作
      reportable segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into three main                   為確定經營分部之基準。具體而言，於
      operating divisions – (i) manufacturing and sale of laminates; (ii)               香港財務報告準則第8號下，本集團之
      properties; and (iii) investments.                                                須予呈報分部分為三個主要經營分部－
                                                                                        (i)製造及銷售覆銅面板；(ii)物業；及(iii)
                                                                                        投資。

      The accounting policies the Group used for segment reporting under               本集團根據香港財務報告準則第8號用
      HKFRS 8 are the same as those used in its HKFRS consolidated                     作分部呈報之會計政策，與其用於香港
      financial statements. Segment results represents the profit earned               財務報告準則之綜合財務報表一致。
      or loss incurred by each segment with certain items not included                 分部業績指除若干項目（未分配企業收
      (unallocated corporate income and expenses, share-based payments                 入及開支、股份形式付款及融資成本）
      and finance costs). This is the measure reported to the CODM for the             外，各分部所賺取的溢利或引致的虧
      purpose of resource allocation and performance assessment.                       損。這是報告給主要營運決策者用於資
                                                                                       源分配和績效評估的措施。

      The provision of LAT is estimated according to the requirements                   計提土地增值稅撥備是按有關中國稅法
      set forth in the relevant PRC tax laws and regulations. LAT has                   及規則要求估算。按土地增值金額（根
      been levied at progressive rates ranging from 30% to 60% on the                   據物業銷售收入扣去指定直接成本）以
      appreciation of land value, represented by the excess of sales                    累進稅率30%至60%基準繳交土地增值
      proceeds of properties over prescribed direct costs. Prescribed                   稅。指定直接成本界定為土地成本，發
      direct costs are defined to include costs of land, development and                展及建築成本，及其他關於房產發展的
      construction costs, as well as certain costs relating to the property             成本。指定直接成本界定為土地成本，
      development. According to the State Administration of Taxation’s                  發展及建築成本，及其他關於房產發
      official circulars, LAT shall be payable provisionally upon sales of              展的成本。按照國家稅務總局之官方公
      the properties, followed by final ascertainment of the gain at the                告，銷售物業時應暫繳土地增值稅，到
      completion of the properties development.                                         房產發展完成後才確認所得收益。

      Under the two-tiered profits tax rates regime of Hong Kong Profits                根據利得稅兩級制或香港利得稅制度，
      Tax, the first HK$2 million of profits of the qualifying group entity will        合資格集團實體的首二百萬港元利潤將
      be taxed at 8.25%, and profits above HK$2 million will be taxed at                按8.25%徵稅，而超過二百萬港元的利
      16.5%. The profits of group entities not qualifying for the two-tiered            潤則須按16.5%徵稅。不符合利得稅兩
      profits tax rates regime will continue to be taxed at a flat rate of              級制的集團實體的利潤將繼續按16.5%
      16.5%. Accordingly, the Hong Kong Profits Tax of the qualifying group             的固定稅率徵稅。因此，合資格集團
      entity is calculated at 8.25% on the first HK$2 million of the estimated          實體首二百萬港元估計應課稅溢利將
      assessable profits and at 16.5% on the estimated assessable profits               按 8.25% 的 稅 率 繳 納 香 港 利 得 稅 ， 而
      above HK$2 million.                                                               二百萬港元以上之估計應課稅溢利則按
                                                                                        16.5%的稅率繳稅。

      prevailing in the relevant jurisdictions.                                         之適用稅率計算。

      The Group leases out various offices under operating leases with                  本集團根據經營租賃出租多個辦公室，
      rentals payable monthly. The leases typically run for a fixed period of           每月收取租金。租賃一般附有介乎1至7
      1 to 7 years (2022: 1 to 7 years).                                                年的固定租期（二零二二年：1至7年）。

      The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of the              本集團不會因租賃安排而承擔外匯風
      lease arrangement, as all leases are denominated in the respective                險，因為所有租賃均以集團實體各自的
      functional currencies of group entities. The lease contracts do not               功能貨幣列值。租約並不包括餘值擔保
      contain residual value guarantee and/or lessee’s option to purchase               及╱或承租人於租期末購買物業的選擇
      the property at the end of lease term.                                            權。

      In determining the fair value of the relevant properties, the                     為釐定相關物業的公平值，管理層就公
      management determines the appropriate valuation techniques and                    平值計量釐定適當的估值技術及輸入數
      inputs for fair value measurements.                                               據。

      The Group engages an independent qualified valuer to perform its                  本集團委聘獨立合資格估值師估值。管
      valuations. The management works closely with the independent                     理層與獨立合資格估值師緊密合作，
      qualified valuer to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and            共同制定模型之適當估值技術及輸入數
      inputs to the model. The persons in charge of financial matters report            據。財務事宜負責人每季向董事匯報管
      the management findings to the Directors every quarter to explain the             理層之發現，闡明資產及負債公平值波
      cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets and liabilities.            動之因由。

      The Group’s investment properties were classified as Level 3 of the               本集團投資物業於二零二三年及二零
      fair value hierarchy as at 31 December 2023 and 2022.                             二二年十二月三十一日分類為第三級公
                                                                                        平值架構級別。

      The fair value of the Group’s investment properties at 31 December                本集團投資物業於二零二三年及二零
      2023 and 2022 had been arrived at on the basis of a valuation carried             二二年十二月三十一日之公平值乃按與
      out by Ravia Global Appraisal Advisory Limited, an independent                    本集團並無關連之獨立合資格估值師瑞
      qualified valuer not connected to the Group. Ravia Global Appraisal               豐環球評估諮詢有限公司進行估值之基
      Advisory Limited is a member of the Hong Kong Institute of Surveyors.             準達致。瑞豐環球評估諮詢有限公司為
      The valuation was determined based on direct comparison method                    香港測量師學會會員。估值乃按直接比

      consideration of timing of reference transaction and property-specific            考交易的時間及物業特定的調整因素後
      adjustment factors. There has been no change from the valuation                   釐定。於過往年度所用之估值技術並無

31. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                        31. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will           資本風險管理本集團管理其資本以確保
      be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to             通過優化債務與股本結存為權益持有人
      stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.             爭取最高回報，使本集團旗下實體能夠
      The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.                   持續經營。本集團之整體策略與過往年
                                                                                        度相同。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings                    誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團之
      and lease liabilities, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity                資本結構包括銀行借貸及租賃負債（扣
      attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued share                    除現金及現金等值項目）及本公司持有
      capital, share premium, reserves and retained profits as disclosed in             人應佔權益（包括已發行股本、股份溢
      the consolidated statement of changes in equity.                                  價、儲備及保留溢利）。



      The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.                董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
      As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and            部分，董事將考慮資本成本，及與各類
      the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the            別資本相關之風險。董事認為，本集團
      Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through           將通過支付股息、發行新股、購回股
      the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as                 份、發行新債或者贖回現有債項，以平
      well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.                 衡整體資本結構。

            perform their obligations at the end of the reporting period                  認金融資產之承擔，則本集團須
            in relation to each class of recognised financial assets is the               承受之最高信貸風險為於綜合財
            carrying amount of those assets as stated in the consolidated                 務狀況表所載該等資產賬面值。
            statement of financial position.

            follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts. In this                   以確保採取跟進措施收回逾期未
            regard, the Directors consider that the Group’s credit risk is                付之債項。就此而言，本公司董
            significantly reduced.                                                        事認為，本集團之信貸風險已大
                                                                                          幅降低。

            Liquidity risk                                                                   流動資金風險
            In the management of the liquidity risk, the Group monitors                      本集團為管理流動資金風險，監
            and maintains a level of cash and cash equivalents deemed                        控現金及現金等值項目的水平，
            adequate by the management to finance the Group’s                                將其維持於管理層認為充足的水
            operations and mitigate the effects of fluctuations in cash                      平，來撥支本集團的營運，並減
            flows. The Directors monitor the utilisation of bank borrowings                  低現金流量波動的影響。於年
            and ensures compliance with loan covenants during the year.                      內，董事監控銀行借貸的使用情
                                                                                             況，確保符合貸款契諾。

            The Group relies on bank borrowings as a significant source                      本集團依賴銀行借貸作為流
            of liquidity. At 31 December 2023, the Group has available                       動資金的主要來源。於二零
            unutilised bank borrowing facilities of HK$8,105,347,000                         二三年十二月三十一日，本
            (2022: HK$7,374,337,000).                                                        集團未動用的銀行信貸額度為

      of the Directors, result in particulars of excessive length.

      the year.                                                                                       證券。
